| the runtime has a harness; a mode-less turn is a command or talks to it | B · C | m80, 10-07 · 10-08 |
| two tiers: TOOLS one low-level op · SKILLS integrated, an Activity where due · every one a Vector | D | 10-08 |
| no external harness: no instance-record slot, nothing in =commons/=; the runtime's harness is authored in the runtime | E | 10-08 |
| the cortex is the runtime's, once | G | m80, 10-07 |
| a mode runs straight in the runtime; the key is =modes= on both rungs, =kernel= dies | H | 10-08 |
| the runtime's harness is its first mode; tooling follows the same way | H | 10-08 |
| one pipeline instantiates a mode on either rung, written as tooling | H | 10-08 |
| that tooling lives in gestalten, not under =lifecycle/= | I | 10-08 |
| a function's home is its first parameter: =(ctx, next)= → =gestalten/shard/= · =(die, next)= → =lifecycle/= · =new Vector().open(…)= → =gestalten/{tools,skills}/<subject>.js= · plain over data → =gestalten/belt/= | K | 10-09 |
| =gestalten/belt/= is typology-deep — no file system, no paladin; =stamp= leaves it | K | 10-09 |

| =forge= dies; the harness pipeline is shards; the trait WIRES and ARMS | J | 10-09 |
| tools and skills: a folder each, the subject in the file name, the barrel FLAT (=tools.buffer=, never =tools.buffer.buffer=); named imports =import { tools, skills, shard }= | J | 10-09 |

Code after (lean): the home's =claiming= throws =thread <id> is in a mode — its message goes to that mode's harness=. REJECTED: answering — two harnesses on one thread.

* the law

- a TOOL is one repository operation: one read, or one write (assign + flush after the reads that validate it). It never calls the cortex, never dispatches, never reads the controller.
- a SKILL composes tools or drives another harness, so it holds the round's controller: =await ctx.controller.proceed()= between steps, =ctx.controller.branch(<step>)= down. One send, one Activity — minted by =activating=; a skill never mints one.
- every tool and skill is a Vector node ={ nature, valence, input }=; the model sees one list, named =absolute.join("_")=.
- one harness, two rungs: =HARNESSED(mode, host)= spreads the host's words FLAT onto ctx (=shard.context.spread(contexts.host, host)=) and binds =mode=; no =daemon= · =runtime= · =host= key on ctx — the ctx IS the composition, declared in =schematics/primitives/contexts.js=; it wires =runtimeShard.harness.*= — =normalizing · claiming · activating · requesting(arming) · chaining · persisting · summarizing · lexicon(cortex)=; every harness has a mode, the home included, so no shard takes one.
- a tool returns a CARD, never a live row: a row's collections reach the turn its result lands in.
- a HOST bears modes: =type · slug · reference · attach · controller · aperture · entities · datamap · twitch · cortex · modes · services · domain · cargo=. Daemon and Runtime both are one; the word lives in the tooling and the trait's parameter, never on ctx. =flatmodes= is =fromm.modes(<modes map>).flat=, never a method.
- a CONTEXT is a flat bag of words, declared one key per export in =subsystems/typology/schematics/primitives/contexts.js= and assembled per environment from those keys (=Aperture · Tool · Harness · Emitter · Twitch · Service=); a group exists only where its keys are minted together (=vector= — the strategy's seven; =host= — the ten =belt.host.seat= places). A trait COMPOSES its context: =shard.context.spread(contexts.host, bearer)= · =bind(word, value)= · =conform(...sets)= (=v.faults= against =contexts.of(...sets)=, throws =ValidationError=). Nearest wins: a trait's own =controller · tools · thread= are never in the host group.
- =reference= is computed the same way for every bearer and every row — =/= the runtime · =/daemon/<slug>= a daemon · =/mode/<type>/<slug>= a mode; =datamap.owned()= compares the row's computed owner reference to =ctx.reference=.
- one pipeline, two rungs: =gestalten.belt.host.{seat, resolve, release, prune}= are plain functions over a host; =lifecycle/daemon= and =lifecycle/runtime= call them as beats. A trait is =(mode, host)=.
- the record key is =modes= on both rungs — ="runtime": { "modes": [...] }= · ="daemons": [{ "modes": [...] }]=; =kernel= dies everywhere it meant that.
- the runtime's harness is a MODE under =runtime.modes=, HARNESSED like any other; every mode aperture sits behind =authenticate · bind=; no route touches composition or the registry (m80's law, kept).
- a function's HOME is its first parameter: =(ctx, next)= → =gestalten/shard/= · =(die, next)= → =lifecycle/= · =new Vector().open(…)= → =gestalten/{tools,skills}/<subject>.js= · plain over data → =gestalten/belt/=. The belt is typology-deep: no file system, no paladin. A trait is one file named after itself; it wires shards and arms tools, it holds no bodies. A barrel re-exports; a tools barrel exports its Vectors FLAT.
- REJECTED: a tier split by "touches the LLM" · an Activity per skill · a home daemon · a beat-authored home harness (superseded by H) · an instance-record harness slot (E) · =fs= · =shell= at home (F6) · a second mode table (H3) · =forge= · =markers.js= · a belt file reading disk.

* design — M0 · the host

The daemon's three beats, re-pointed. Every block below is the 10-08 SKETCH; =* tangle — THE LAND …= (10-09, PROVEN) is the truth where they differ — =mint= became =seat=, =ctx.host= never happened (H1 ruled), =prune= joined the host tooling.

#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/runtime/gestalten/host/index.js // -- @beef /belt/host.js
import { Mode, steer } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { stagger } from "../belt/index.js";

export const HOST = ["slug", "reference", "attach", "controller", "aperture", "entities", "datamap", "twitch", "cortex", "modes", "services", "domain"];

export const host = (bearer) => {
  const missing = HOST.filter((word) => !(word in bearer));
  if (missing.length) throw new Error(`host ${bearer.slug} lacks ${missing.join(" ")}`);
  return bearer;
};

export const flatmodes = (bearer) => Object.values(bearer.modes).flatMap((type) => Object.values(type));

export const mint = (bearer, masks) => {
  for (const mask of masks) {
    (bearer.modes[mask.manifest.type] ??= {})[mask.manifest.slug] = Object.assign(new Mode(mask), { stdout: bearer.controller.stdout.branch(mask.reference.absolute) });
  }
  return bearer;
};

export const resolve = async (bearer, execution, traits) => {
  for (const mode of flatmodes(bearer)) await steer.dispatch.execute(execution, { mask: mode.module, mode, host: bearer, traits });
  await bearer.datamap.shard.scope(() => Promise.all(flatmodes(bearer).flatMap((mode) => mode.finalizers).map((finalize) => finalize())));
  for (const mode of flatmodes(bearer)) bearer.aperture.branch(mode.reference.nature, mode.aperture);
};

export const release = async (bearer) => {
  for (const mode of flatmodes(bearer)) {
    for (const terminate of mode.terminators ?? []) await terminate();
    mode.stdout.close();
  }
};
#+END_SRC

The cortex lexicon (RULED 10-09, K): =POST /daemon/<slug>/cortex/<type>/<via>=, the body is the request; the ROUND envelope dies, and with it any round shard.

#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/runtime/lifecycle/daemon/aperture/cortex.js — after
export const cortex = async (die, next) => {
  if (die.daemon.cortex) {
    for (const type of ["dialogue", "object", "choice", "verbatim"])
      die.daemon.aperture.branch("/cortex").branch(type)
        .open("/render", (ctx) => die.daemon.cortex.hallucinate[type].render(ctx.input))
        .open("/stream", (ctx) => die.daemon.cortex.hallucinate[type].stream(ctx.input));
  }
  await next();
};
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC js
// subsystems/typology/gestalten/belt/cortex.js — moved from shape/cortex.js; a provider's faculty calls the lexicon
const faculty = connection.branch(meta.type);
if (meta.via.includes("render")) via.render = (request) => faculty.call("/render", request);
if (meta.via.includes("stream")) via.stream = (request) => faculty.observe("/stream", { method: "POST", body: request });
#+END_SRC

The typology shard namespace and the runtime's collide on the word =shard= in =harnessed.js=. RULED 10-09: =import { shard as runtimeShard } from "../../../gestalten/index.js"= at file level; typology's =shard= keeps its name.

#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/runtime/lifecycle/runtime/aperture/userspace.js — the seven blocks as one table (2h); activity stays hand-written
const own = (ctx) => ({ user: ctx.user.id });
const viaThread = (ctx) => ({ thread: { user: ctx.user.id } });
const ROUTES = [
  ["terminal", entities.terminal, own, { scope: own, filter: "user" }],
  ["intent", entities.intent, null, {}],
  ["thread", entities.thread, own, {}],
  ["buffer", entities.buffer, viaThread, { scope: own }],
  ["turn", entities.turn, viaThread, { scope: own }],
  ["note", kernel.note, null, { scope: own }],
];
for (const [kind, repository, scope, reactive] of ROUTES) {
  const branch = owned.branch(`/${kind}`);
  if (scope) branch.use(shard.datamap.scope(scope));
  branch.graft(shard.datamap.repository(repository)).graft(shard.datamap.reactive(repository, twitch, reactive));
}
#+END_SRC

=stamp= moves to =lifecycle/mode/traits/dataset.js= as an export (it reads disk through paladin — not belt); =datasink.js= and =mode/resolution.js= import it from there. =assemble= stays in the belt for now — beef: /"feels weird"/, no ruling; its home is QUESTIONED under deferred.

* design — the rotation · a mode on either rung (L, 10-09)

Read 10-09, every file: the eleven traits (=lifecycle/mode/traits/*.js=), the three mode beats, both executions, =runtime/resolution.attach=, paladin =ground= · =wire=, the Mode class, =Buffer.ts= · =Terminal.ts= · =Mode.ts=. What a trait touches on its bearer — the whole =daemon.= surface, grouped:

| group | words | readers |
|-------+-------+---------|
| seat | =slug · reference · attach · url · mountpoint= | =carry= (FRAUGHT · MOUNTED) =traits/index.js:43= · =runtime/resolution.attach= · =population.modes= (stdout branch) |
| data | =entities · datamap · twitch= | APPLICATION · GENERATIVE · EMITTER · INTENTED · DATASET · DATASINK · HARNESSED · the mode beats |
| mind | =cortex= | HARNESSED |
| peers | =flatmodes() · services · domain= | AGENTIC · INTENTED · the armory · =summary.thread= |
| process | =controller= | =population.modes= |

Five groups = the =HOST= word list of =* design — M0=, measured rather than asserted. The runtime holds =data · mind · process= today (=prototypes/runtime.js=); it lacks the seat and the peers — R3 and M0 add them.

What differs between the rungs is the SEAT alone. A buffer is =host.entities.buffer.create({ mode: mode.entity.id, thread })= on both (=application.js:15= · =generative.js:81=); its row filters by =thread.user= (=Buffer.ts:60=), never by daemon; =Terminal.daemon= is already nullable (=Terminal.ts:36=). A bundle compiles at =mode.bundles= (the seat's =<mountpoint>/bundles/<type>/<slug>=) and is served under =/attached/bundle= + =host.reference= + =mode.reference= (=runtime/resolution.js:34–37=); cargo under =/attached/cargo= + =host.reference.nature=. The daemon's =attach= is already the RUNTIME's reach + =/attached= (=paladin/lifecycle/resolve.js:37=) — no daemon segment in it. So the rotation collapses to one sentence: the runtime is a host whose reference is the root, and every formula degenerates to the runtime's own aperture.

/"rewrite the tangle. full land."/ — ONE section, cut 10-09 against the repo AS IT STANDS (=scratchpad/rig9=, copied after the sibling's registry-record landing of 14:37), producing =scratchpad/rig8=. It supersedes the two proven sections it replaced: the runtime rewrite, and the flatten. Their history is in =* changelog=.

How =rig8= was made:
- base =rig2= (the copy the work began on), mine =rig3=, theirs the repo;
- three-way merge with =diff3 -m=, per file. 101 files were untouched by the sibling and take mine verbatim. 5 were drifted (=commons/instances/hello-world/tools/doctor.js= · =subsystems/paladin/prototypes/ledger/registry.js= · =subsystems/paladin/tests/ledger.parts.test.js= · =subsystems/typology/schematics/primitives/instance.js= · =systems/runtime/tests/runtime/hello.test.js=) and merged with 0 conflicts — the sibling's =tapped()= record and =Tapped= schematic are kept.

What lands, by ruling (=* decisions=):
- the pass (J · K) — =belt/traits.js= · =stamp= into =dataset.js= · one file per trait · the cortex lexicon =/cortex/<type>/<via>= · typology =belt/cortex.js= · =shard/cargo.js= · the userspace table · =die.traits=;
- the host (H · I · L) — =gestalten/belt/host.js= =seat · resolve · release · prune=; =modes= on both rungs through paladin; =Mode.daemon= nullable; the =RUNTIME= trait;
- the flat ctx (ctx) — typology =schematics/primitives/contexts.js= (37 keys, =vector · host=, six contexts), =shard.context.{lift, spread, conform}=, =fromm.modes(map).flat=; no =ctx.daemon= · =ctx.runtime= · =flatmodes=;
- traits at the top (ctx) — =systems/runtime/lifecycle/mode/traits/= → =systems/runtime/traits/=; the package exports =traits=;
- the data traits out of the runtime's catalog (ctx) — =const { DATASET, DATASINK, ...admitted } = traits=;
- a declared trait its host does not offer is LOGGED at boot (/"yes of course. log"/) — =[trait] <type>/<slug> declares <TRAIT>, which <host reference> does not offer= in =lifecycle/mode/resolution.js=; the hello probe declares =DATASET= and the test reads the line;
- the registry's =kernel= key everywhere it is the daemon record's (7 =instance.viva.json= · 2 test dies) — the census of the first cut caught four registry tests red on it;
- the runtime at =/runtime/<slug>= (R6, /"yes. go"/; the slug ruling 10-09) — paladin writes =instance.runtime.reference= =/runtime/<slug>= and its =url= (=resolve.js= =root()=; =/runtime/runtime= on the default slug), =run.js= hands =slug= · =reference= to =Runtime=; the root server is =die.aperture= on the die; =die.runtime.aperture= is the runtime-as-host, mounted at its reference by =expose= the way each daemon is mounted at =/daemon/<slug>=; runtime modes are served at =/runtime/<slug>/mode/<type>/<slug>=; runtime cargo at =/attached/cargo/runtime/<slug>/…= (the root catch-all that could shadow daemon cargo is gone); =/metadata/modes= lists absolute references; =datamap.owned()= reads a =daemon NULL= row as the host whose reference starts =/runtime/=;

** =subsystems/typology/gestalten/belt/cortex.js= — new
#+BEGIN_SRC js
// subsystems/typology/gestalten/belt/cortex.js
export const strip = (cortex) =>
  [...cortex.faculties.values()].flat().map((faculty) => ({
    type: faculty.type,
    tune: faculty.tune,
    context: faculty.context,
    ...(faculty.options !== undefined && { options: faculty.options }),
    ...(faculty.choices !== undefined && { choices: faculty.choices }),
    channels: faculty.channels,
    ...(faculty.provider && { provider: faculty.provider }),
    ...(faculty.config && { config: faculty.config }),
    via: Object.keys(faculty.via),
  }));

export const wire = (connection, stripped = []) => stripped.map((meta) => ({ ...meta, via: avenues(connection.branch(meta.type), meta) }));

const avenues = (faculty, meta) => {
  const via = {};
  if (meta.via.includes("render")) via.render = (request) => faculty.call("/render", { tune: meta.tune, ...request });
  if (meta.via.includes("stream")) via.stream = (request) => faculty.observe("/stream", { method: "POST", body: { tune: meta.tune, ...request } });
  return via;
};
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC js
// subsystems/typology/schematics/primitives/contexts.js
import { v } from "../v.js";
import { Mountpoint } from "./instance.js";
import { Hallucination, Policy, Turn } from "./hallucination.js";

export const input = { input: v.unknown().desc("The step's payload, request.body. Example: { text: 'ciao' }") };
export const output = { output: v.unknown().optional().desc("The step's result, response.body; set by the effect. Example: { id: 42 }") };
export const request = { request: v.unknown().desc("is.Request — { body, url, method, raw? }") };
export const response = { response: v.unknown().desc("is.Response — { body, status, headers }") };
export const params = { params: v.record(v.string(), v.string()).desc("Path parameters matched off the signal. Example: { slug: 'dojo' }") };
export const steps = { steps: v.array(v.unknown()).desc("The matched trajectory, root first") };
export const signal = { signal: v.unknown().desc("The Signal that fired. Example: new Signal('/dialogue/render')") };

export const entities = { entities: v.record(v.string(), v.unknown()).desc("The host's repositories by entity name. Example: { note, mode } on the runtime; { literal, em, buffer } on a daemon") };
export const call = { call: v.unknown().optional().desc("The host's aperture as a callable object, shape.object(aperture). Example: ctx.call.rules.position({ fen })") };
export const cortex = { cortex: v.unknown().optional().desc("The host's Cortex, a Pool of faculties. Example: ctx.cortex.findOne({ type: 'dialogue', tune: 'fast', via: 'stream' })") };
export const modes = { modes: v.record(v.string(), v.record(v.string(), v.unknown())).desc("Sibling modes on the host, modes.<type>.<slug>. Example: ctx.modes.game.dojo") };
export const services = { services: v.record(v.string(), v.unknown()).desc("Live services on the host by slug. Example: ctx.services.nlp") };
export const statics = { statics: v.unknown().optional().desc("The host's declared statics. Example: { language: 'it' }") };
export const domain = { domain: v.unknown().optional().desc("The host's domain module, if any") };
export const controller = { controller: v.unknown().desc("is.Controller — the host's stdout branch and signal") };
export const mountpoint = { mountpoint: Mountpoint.desc("Where the host is mounted. Example: '/daemon/chess' · '/' on the runtime") };
export const reference = { reference: v.unknown().desc("is.Path — the host's computed reference, <type>/<slug>. Example: new Path('/daemon/chess') · new Path('/') on the runtime") };
export const cargo = { cargo: v.unknown().desc("The host's Cargo — every fraught mode's catalog by slug. Example: ctx.cargo['sentences/ho-fame.mp3']") };

export const mode = { mode: v.unknown().desc("The live Mode the step runs in. Example: ctx.mode.id · ctx.mode.application.buffer") };
export const user = { user: v.unknown().desc("The authenticated user, lighthouse-signed. Example: { id: 'u1', roles: ['player'] }") };
export const lighthouse = { lighthouse: v.unknown().desc("The Lighthouse that signed this request") };
export const service = { service: v.unknown().desc("The live Service a tool branch belongs to") };
export const mask = { mask: v.unknown().desc("The service's record mask, { module, statics?, secrets? }") };

export const thread = { thread: v.unknown().desc("The Thread this exchange belongs to. Example: { id: 't9', mode: 7 }") };
export const turn = { turn: Turn.desc("The user Turn chained for this request") };
export const hallucination = { hallucination: v.union([Hallucination.dialogue, Hallucination.verbatim, Hallucination.speech, Hallucination.choice]).desc("The request under construction, cortex.hallucinate.<type>.<via>(ctx.hallucination)") };
export const policy = { policy: v.union([Policy.dialogue, Policy.verbatim, Policy.speech, Policy.choice]).desc("The record's policy, lifted off the hallucination") };
export const tools = { tools: v.unknown().desc("The armed Vector the model may call, never domain.tools") };
export const intelligent = { intelligent: v.boolean().desc("The mode's harness.intelligent flag, lifted") };
export const vocal = { vocal: v.boolean().desc("The mode's harness.vocal flag, lifted") };
export const activity = { activity: v.unknown().desc("The Activity row this exchange is written to") };
export const screen = { screen: v.unknown().desc("A guide harness's current screen. Example: { id: 'intro', fields: [] }") };

export const pool = { pool: v.unknown().desc("The emitter's Pool; yield into it. Example: ctx.pool.add(literal)") };
export const scope = { scope: v.record(v.string(), v.unknown()).desc("The datamap scope narrowing reads and stamping writes. Example: { user: 'u1' }") };

export const optional = (set) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(set).map(([key, schema]) => [key, schema.optional()]));
export const of = (...sets) => v.object(Object.assign({}, ...sets));

export const vector = { ...input, ...output, ...request, ...response, ...params, ...steps, ...signal };
export const host = { ...entities, ...call, ...cortex, ...modes, ...services, ...statics, ...domain, ...mountpoint, ...reference, ...cargo };

export const Aperture = { ...vector, ...host, ...mode, ...optional(user) };
export const Tool = { ...vector, ...host, ...mode, ...user, ...optional(thread), ...optional(service) };
export const Harness = { ...vector, ...host, ...mode, ...user, ...optional(thread), ...optional(turn), ...hallucination, ...tools, ...controller, ...intelligent, ...vocal, ...optional(activity), ...optional(screen) };
export const Emitter = { ...vector, ...host, ...mode, ...pool, ...optional(thread), ...optional(user) };
export const Twitch = { ...vector, ...entities, ...call, ...cortex, ...services, ...statics, ...domain, ...mountpoint, ...reference, ...optional(scope), ...optional(mode) };
export const Service = { ...vector, ...service, ...mask, ...tools };
#+END_SRC

** =systems/runtime/gestalten/belt/host.js= — new
#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/runtime/gestalten/belt/host.js
import { fromm, Mode, steer } from "@vivalence/typology";

export const HOST = ["type", "slug", "reference", "attach", "controller", "aperture", "entities", "datamap", "twitch", "cortex", "modes", "services", "domain"];

export const host = (bearer) => {
  const missing = HOST.filter((word) => !(word in bearer));
  if (missing.length) throw new Error(`host ${bearer.slug} lacks ${missing.join(" ")}`);
  return bearer;
};

export const seat = (bearer, masks) => {
  for (const mask of masks) {
    (bearer.modes[mask.manifest.type] ??= {})[mask.manifest.slug] = Object.assign(new Mode(mask), { stdout: bearer.controller.stdout.branch(mask.reference.absolute) });
  }
  return bearer;
};

export const resolve = async (bearer, execution, traits) => {
  for (const mode of fromm.modes(bearer.modes).flat) {
    await steer.dispatch.execute(execution, { mask: mode.module, mode, host: bearer, traits });
  }
  await bearer.datamap.shard.scope(() => Promise.all(fromm.modes(bearer.modes).flat.flatMap((mode) => mode.finalizers).map((finalize) => finalize())));
  for (const mode of fromm.modes(bearer.modes).flat) {
    bearer.aperture.branch(mode.reference.nature, mode.aperture);
  }
};

export const release = async (bearer) => {
  for (const mode of fromm.modes(bearer.modes).flat) {
    for (const terminate of mode.terminators ?? []) await terminate();
    mode.stdout.close();
  }
};

const typeSlug = (item) => `${item.type}:${item.slug}`;

const orphans = (em, rows, keep, keyOf, label) => {
  for (const row of rows) {
    if (keep.has(keyOf(row))) continue;
    em.remove(row);
    console.log(`pruned ${label}:`, row.slug);
  }
};

export const prune = async (bearer) => {
  await bearer.datamap.shard.scope(async () => {
    const modes = fromm.modes(bearer.modes).flat;
    const installed = new Set(modes.map((mode) => typeSlug(mode.manifest)));
    orphans(bearer.entities.em, await bearer.entities.mode.find({}), installed, typeSlug, "mode");
    await bearer.entities.em.flush();
    for (const mode of modes) {
      const slugs = new Set((mode.module.dataset?.intent ?? []).map((intent) => intent.slug));
      orphans(bearer.entities.em, await bearer.entities.intent.find({ mode: mode.entity.id }, { filters: false }), slugs, (intent) => intent.slug, "intent");
    }
    await bearer.entities.em.flush();
  });
};
#+END_SRC

** =systems/runtime/gestalten/belt/traits.js= — new
#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/runtime/gestalten/belt/traits.js
import { is, traits as trait } from "@vivalence/typology";

export async function stagger(bearer, context, traits) {
  const finalizers = [];
  for (const name of bearer.manifest.traits) {
    const result = await traits[name]?.(bearer, context);
    if (is.fn(result)) finalizers.push(result);
    else if (is.object(result)) {
      if (is.fn(result.finalize)) finalizers.push(result.finalize);
      if (is.fn(result.terminate)) {
        (bearer.terminators ??= []).push(result.terminate);
      }
    }
  }
  return finalizers;
}

const named = (name, Class) => Object.defineProperty(Class, "name", { value: name });
const capitalize = (word) => (word ?? "runtime").charAt(0).toUpperCase() + (word ?? "runtime").slice(1).replace(/-(\w)/g, (_, c) => c.toUpperCase());

export const gather = (kernel, domain, modes, kind) =>
  trait.collide(
    [...kernel[kind], ...(domain?.traits?.[kind] ?? []), ...modes.flatMap((mode) => mode.traits?.[kind] ?? [])].filter(
      (held, index, all) => all.findIndex((other) => other.name === held.name) === index,
    ),
  );

export const mint = ({ slug, kinds, traits }) =>
  Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(kinds).map(([kind, descriptor]) => {
      const held = traits[kind] ?? [];
      const schematics = Object.fromEntries(held.filter((each) => each.schematic).map((each) => [each.name, each.schematic]));
      const Repository = named(
        `${capitalize(slug)}${capitalize(kind)}Repository`,
        class extends descriptor.repository {
          scope(options = {}) {
            return { ...options, filters: { ...(options.filters ?? {}), daemon: { daemon: slug } } };
          }
          find(where, options) { return super.find(where, this.scope(options)); }
          findOne(where, options) { return super.findOne(where, this.scope(options)); }
          findOneOrFail(where, options) { return super.findOneOrFail(where, this.scope(options)); }
          findAll(options) { return super.findAll(this.scope(options)); }
          findAndCount(where, options) { return super.findAndCount(where, this.scope(options)); }
          findByCursor(where, options) { return super.findByCursor(where, this.scope(options)); }
          count(where, options) { return super.count(where, this.scope(options)); }
          nativeUpdate(where, data, options) { return super.nativeUpdate(where, data, this.scope(options)); }
          nativeDelete(where, options) { return super.nativeDelete(where, this.scope(options)); }
          create(data, options) { return super.create({ ...data, daemon: slug }, options); }
          upsert(data, options) { return super.upsert({ ...data, daemon: slug }, options); }
          unique(query) { return { ...super.unique(query), daemon: slug }; }
          claim(row, name) { return trait.claim(row, name, schematics); }
        },
      );
      trait.repository(Repository, held);
      Object.defineProperties(Repository.prototype, { daemon: { value: slug }, schematics: { value: schematics } });
      return [kind, { repository: Repository, traits: held, schematics }];
    }),
  );

export const strike = (minted, em, kinds) => {
  const struck = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(minted).map(([kind, held]) => [kind, new held.repository(em, kinds[kind].entity.name)]));
  for (const [kind, held] of Object.entries(minted)) {
    trait.entity(kinds[kind].entity, held.traits, struck[kind].daemon);
    Object.defineProperty(struck[kind], "entities", { value: struck });
  }
  return struck;
};
#+END_SRC

** =systems/runtime/gestalten/shard/cargo.js= — new
#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/runtime/gestalten/shard/cargo.js
import { fromm } from "@vivalence/typology";

const CACHE = "no-cache";

export const serve = (fraught) => async (input, ctx) => {
  const query = fromm.params(ctx.params).path.absolute.replace(/^[/]/, "");
  for (const mode of fraught()) {
    const entry = mode.freight.resolve(query);
    if (!entry) continue;
    const filePath = mode.freight.path.branch("/" + entry.path).absolute;
    const stat = await Deno.stat(filePath);
    const etag = `"${stat.size}-${stat.mtime?.getTime() ?? 0}"`;
    const headers = { "content-type": entry.type, "cache-control": CACHE, etag, "accept-ranges": "bytes" };
    if (ctx.request.headers.get("if-none-match") === etag) return new Response(null, { status: 304, headers });
    const bytes = await Deno.readFile(filePath);
    const range = /^bytes=(\d*)-(\d*)$/.exec(ctx.request.headers.get("range") ?? "");
    if (!range) return new Response(bytes, { status: 200, headers });
    const start = range[1] ? Number(range[1]) : Math.max(0, bytes.length - Number(range[2]));
    const end = range[1] && range[2] ? Math.min(Number(range[2]), bytes.length - 1) : bytes.length - 1;
    if (start > end || start >= bytes.length) {
      return new Response(null, { status: 416, headers: { ...headers, "content-range": `bytes */${bytes.length}` } });
    }
    return new Response(bytes.subarray(start, end + 1), { status: 206, headers: { ...headers, "content-range": `bytes ${start}-${end}/${bytes.length}` } });
  }
  ctx.response.status = 404;
};
#+END_SRC

** =systems/runtime/tests/host.test.js= — new
#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/runtime/tests/host.test.js
import { Aperture, Controller, fromm, Path, specimen, Vector } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { gestalten } from "@vivalence/runtime";

const { host } = gestalten.belt;

const bearer = (type) => ({
  type,
  slug: type === "runtime" ? "runtime" : "probe",
  reference: new Path(type === "runtime" ? "/" : "/daemon/probe"),
  attach: null,
  controller: new Controller(),
  aperture: new Aperture(),
  entities: {},
  datamap: { shard: { scope: (work) => work() } },
  twitch: null,
  cortex: null,
  modes: {},
  services: {},
  domain: null,
});

const mask = (type, slug) => ({ manifest: { type, slug, traits: [] }, reference: new Path(`/mode/${type}/${slug}`) });

const execution = (seen) =>
  new Vector().affect(async (die) => {
    seen.push([die.host.type, die.mode.manifest.slug]);
    die.mode.aperture = new Aperture().open("/ping", () => die.mode.manifest.slug);
    die.mode.finalizers = [() => seen.push(["finalized", die.mode.manifest.slug])];
    die.mode.terminators = [() => seen.push(["terminated", die.mode.manifest.slug])];
  });

specimen.describe("gestalten.belt.host — one way to bear modes on either rung", () => {
  specimen.it("host() admits a bearer that has every HOST word and names the missing ones", () => {
    specimen.expect(host.host(bearer("daemon")).type).toBe("daemon");
    specimen.expect(host.host(bearer("runtime")).type).toBe("runtime");
    const { cortex, twitch, ...lacking } = bearer("runtime");
    specimen.expect(() => host.host(lacking)).toThrow("host runtime lacks twitch cortex");
  });

  for (const type of ["daemon", "runtime"]) {
    specimen.it(`${type}: seat → resolve → release — the same calls, the mode read through die.host`, async () => {
      const held = host.host(bearer(type));
      host.seat(held, [mask("game", "board"), mask("home", "lobby")]);
      specimen.expect(fromm.modes(held.modes).flat.map((mode) => mode.manifest.slug)).toEqual(["board", "lobby"]);
      specimen.expect(held.modes.game.board.stdout).toBeDefined();

      const seen = [];
      await host.resolve(held, execution(seen), {});
      specimen.expect(seen).toEqual([[type, "board"], [type, "lobby"], ["finalized", "board"], ["finalized", "lobby"]]);
      specimen.expect(held.aperture.branch("/mode/game/board/ping").effect?.()).toBe("board");

      await host.release(held);
      specimen.expect(seen.slice(-2)).toEqual([["terminated", "board"], ["terminated", "lobby"]]);
    });
  }
});
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/runtime/traits/exposed.js
import { shape } from "@vivalence/typology";

export const EXPOSED = (mode) => {
  if (!mode.aperture) {
    console.warn(`[EXPOSED] ${mode.manifest.type}/${mode.manifest.slug} has no aperture`);
    return;
  }
  return () => {
    mode.call = shape.proxy(mode.aperture);
  };
};
#+END_SRC

** =systems/runtime/traits/fraught.js= — new
#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/runtime/traits/fraught.js
import { Freight } from "@vivalence/typology";
import paladin from "@vivalence/paladin";
import { join } from "@std/path";

export const carry = async (mode, host, root) => {
  await paladin.state.dir(root);
  const files = await paladin.find.walk(/./, mode.statics?.ignore)(root);
  mode.freight = new Freight(root).stow(files.map((file) => file.absolute.slice(root.length + 1)));
  mode.freight.withUrl(host.attach.branch("/cargo").branch(host.reference.nature));
  mode.freight.receive = async (path, bytes) => {
    await paladin.state.store(`${root}/${path}`, bytes);
    return mode.freight.admit(path).resolve(path);
  };
  mode.aperture.open("/freight", () => mode.freight.catalog);
};

export const FRAUGHT = (mode, host) => carry(mode, host, join(mode.module.source.dirname, mode.module.freight.path.nature));
#+END_SRC

 export const bind = (key, value) => async (ctx, next) => {
   ctx[key] = value;
…
 };

-// patch: nested object assign.
+export const lift = (set, bearer) => Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(set).map((word) => [word, bearer[word]]));

-// export const fold = (vector, apply) => {vector.use(async (ctx, next) => (apply(ctx), next())); return vector;};
+export const spread = (set, bearer) => async (ctx, next) => {
+  Object.assign(ctx, lift(set, bearer));
+  await next();
+};

+export const conform = (...sets) => {
+  const schema = contexts.of(...sets);
+  return async (ctx, next) => {
+    const faults = v.faults(schema, ctx);
+    if (faults.length) throw new ValidationError(faults, ctx.signal);
+    await next();
+  };
+};
+
#+END_SRC

// systems/runtime/traits/dataset.js:11

 const CHUNK = 100;
+
+const installer = Deno.readTextFile(new URL(import.meta.url));
+
+export const stamp = async (mode) => {
+  const dataset = new Dataset(mode.module.dataset ?? {});
+  const mount = mode.module.source;
+  const files = [];
+  for (const sources of Object.values(dataset.sources)) {
+    for (const source of sources) {
+      if (source.load) {
+        files.push(["load", source.stamp ? String(await source.stamp(mode)) : ""]);
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (source.rows) {
+        files.push(["rows", JSON.stringify(source.rows)]);
+        continue;
+      }
+      const at = `${mount.dirname}/${source.walk ?? source.read}`;
+      if (source.walk) {
+        for (const file of await paladin.find.walk(/./)(at)) {
+          files.push([file.absolute, await Deno.readTextFile(file.absolute).catch(() => "")]);
+        }
+      } else {
+        files.push([at, await Deno.readTextFile(at).catch(() => "")]);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return files
+    .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : 1))
+    .reduce((folded, [path, text]) => hash.string(folded + path + text), hash.string(await installer));
+};

* the clients — anima · ghost on m80 · m81 (campaign 10-09)

Strand O. Both clients' suites run on the repo (=scratchpad/rig9=) and on THE LAND (=rig8=), serially, =HOME= empty (=scratchpad/census-clients.sh=). A live probe then boots the land's runtime and replays anima's mount sequence route by route (=rig11=, a clone of =rig8=, so the land stays byte-equal to this quest). THE LAND's own census never ran either client.

** measured

| suite | files | repo | land, first cut | land, fixed |
|-------+-------+------+-----------------+-------------|
| anima | 39 | 37·2 | 36·3 | 37·2 |
| ghost | 13 | 13·0 | 12·1 | 13·0 |
| the probe | 1 | — | 9 steps green | — |

- =systems/anima/tests/dock/stage.mount.test.js= — pre-existing, filed (=known-issues.org=, "the rendered words carry no status word").
- =systems/anima/tests/interactive/activity.test.js= — pre-existing on the repo, filed 10-09: the stdout tail reads =fault · close= where it expects =resume · stop · close=.
- =systems/anima/tests/terminals.remote.test.js= — THE LAND's. The test mounts its roster on =house.runtime.aperture=, and R6 made that the runtime-as-host, served under =/runtime=. =/metadata/datamap= then 404s. Fixed in the tangle (=house.aperture=); the replay on a fresh copy gives 132 files byte-equal, 308 hunks, 0 missed.
- =systems/ghost/tests/strata.wet.test.js= — "two shells select two instances in parallel" is a race already filed (=known-issues.org= =ledger-doctor-reaps-a-sibling-shell-mid-boot=: =ledger/doctor= reaps the sibling shell's session file). It flips ok/FAILED: green in the repo's census, red in two of three censuses of the land, then red 3/3 on BOTH the repo and the land, run alone. First called "contention" here after 2/2 green runs, which was wrong; the 3-and-3 run corrected it.

** the probe — anima's mount sequence against the land

#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/anima/tests/runtime.contract.test.js (scratchpad/rig11 — not tangled, C5)
it("GAP — the runtime host serves no metadata: what /metadata/modes advertises, and what a daemon serves, 404s under /runtime", async () => {
  const codes = {};
  for (const path of ["/runtime/mode/home/probe/metadata/manifest", "/runtime/mode/home/probe/metadata/aperture", "/runtime/metadata/manifest", "/runtime/metadata/cortex", "/runtime/metadata/aperture", "/runtime/status"]) {
    codes[path] = await world.status(path, access);
  }
  expect(Object.values(codes)).toEqual([404, 404, 404, 404, 404, 404]);
});
#+END_SRC

| step | verdict |
|------+---------|
| =/metadata/datamap= · =/metadata/daemons= (the lighthouse dataspace) | HOLDS |
| the daemon dossier's calls at origin + roster reference | HOLDS |
| =cortex.wire(daemon.branch("/cortex"), stripped)= | HOLDS |
| =shape.connection.wire(daemon, aperture).userspace= | GAP (=undefined=) |
| a runtime-level thread takes a daemon mode's id | HOLDS (one datamap) |
| =/daemon/hello/mode/demo/hello-world/metadata/manifest= | HOLDS |
| =/metadata/modes= | HOLDS: the runtime rung only, absolute |
| =/runtime/metadata/*= · =/runtime/status= · =/runtime/mode/<t>/<s>/metadata/*= | GAP (404 with a token) |
| =/runtime/mode/home/probe/ping= | HOLDS (={ reference: "/runtime" }=) |

** findings

| # | surface | side | since | kind |
|---+---------+------+-------+------|
| 1 | cortex envelope → lexicon | anima | m81 | BREAK, fixed in the land |
| 2 | roster on =house.runtime.aperture= | anima test | m81 R6 | BREAK, fixed in the land |
| 3 | =daemon.kernel= | ghost | m81 H | BREAK, fixed in the land |
| 4 | an activity's =stdin= · =stdout= through =daemon.call.userspace= | anima | m80 | BREAK, live |
| 5 | the interactive client wires =call= from the ROOT aperture | anima test | m80 | BLIND to 4 |
| 6 | a userspace dataspace per daemon | anima | m80 | FRICTION |
| 7 | a thread minted and re-moded through a daemon | anima | m80 | FRICTION |
| 8 | a mode's reference derived from =ctx.daemon= | anima | m81 | GAP |
| 9 | the runtime rung invisible: no =/metadata/modes=, no home | anima | m81 | GAP |
| 10 | the runtime host serves no metadata | runtime | m81 R6 | GAP |
| 11 | two listing shapes for =/metadata/modes= | runtime | m81 R6 | FRICTION |
| 12 | paladin seats the runtime at =/= | paladin | m81 R6 | STALE |
| 13 | the doctor never lists =runtime.modes= | ghost | m81 H | GAP |
| 14 | the address prototype: two segments, a literal ="home"= | ghost test | m81 | FRICTION |
| 15 | fixtures write =instance.viva.js= | ghost tests | m80 | STALE |

1. =systems/anima/src/typology/entities/daemon/dossier.js:143= — =shape.cortex.wire= posted ={ type, tune, request }= to =/cortex/render=; the land posts to =/cortex/<type>/render= through typology =cortex.wire=.
2. =systems/anima/tests/terminals.remote.test.js:32= — see =** measured=.
3. =systems/ghost/trajectories/instance/doctor.js:66= — =daemon.kernel ?? []= is always empty once paladin writes =modes=; the land renames it. Before the land the doctor silently shows no mode seats.
4. =systems/anima/src/typology/entities/activity.js:80-81=:
   #+BEGIN_SRC js
   // systems/anima/src/typology/entities/activity.js:80
   ctx.entity.stdin = ctx.daemon.call.userspace.entities.activity[id].stdin;
   #+END_SRC
   =daemon.call= is wired from =/daemon/<slug>/metadata/aperture= (=dossier.js:118=). The daemon's aperture has no =/userspace= since m80, so =.userspace= is =undefined= and every activity cast throws (probe step 4).
5. =systems/anima/tests/interactive/activity/client.js:20= wires =call= from the root's =/metadata/aperture=, which holds =/userspace=. The test passes the path that production breaks.
6. =dossier.js:24-33,73-78= — each daemon builds its own =Dataspace= holding thread · buffer · turn · intent · activity, all pointing at the ONE =/userspace/entities/*=. With N daemons every thread is fetched and subscribed N times, and =thread.daemon= is whichever daemon cast it.
7. =systems/anima/src/app/panels/c/cards/threads.js:13-30= — =mint(terminals, daemon, mode)= · =remode= write through =daemon.entities.thread=; =Threads.svelte:19-71= picks a daemon first. The runtime needs none of it (probe step 5: one datamap, a thread takes any mode's id).
8. =systems/anima/src/typology/entities/mode/mode.js:61-64= — =reference= · =connection= · =link= are =ctx.daemon.*.branch("/mode/<t>/<s>")=. A runtime mode has no daemon.
9. =grep -rnE "metadata/modes|['\"\`]/runtime" systems/anima/src= → 0. Anima never reads the runtime rung, so the home mode (H4) has no client.
10. =systems/runtime/lifecycle/runtime/resolution.js:85-93= advertises =metadata: /runtime/<slug>/mode/<t>/<s>/metadata=, and nothing serves it. The daemon's =lifecycle/daemon/aperture/metadata.js:4-49= (manifest · statics · cargo · datamap · aperture · cortex · modes, plus a per-mode =/metadata= tree) has no runtime twin. =expose= gives each daemon =/status= · =/manifest= · =/batch= (=resolution.js:76-81=) and the runtime none. The host is one abstraction up to its aperture and two at its metadata.
11. The daemon's =/metadata/modes= rows carry =metadata= and no =reference= (=metadata.js:14-21=); the runtime's carry both (=resolution.js:85-93=).
12. =subsystems/paladin/lifecycle/resolve.js:48= =runtime.reference ??= "/"= and =:95-100= a runtime mode's =url= = =<origin>/mode/<t>/<s>=, while =systems/runtime/prototypes/runtime.js:6= said =/runtime= (resolved: C3, then the slug ruling — paladin writes =/runtime/<slug>=, =Runtime= reads it). 0 readers (=grep -rnE 'runtime\??\.reference|mask\.reference'= → host.js · Process.ts read the DAEMON's and the mode's), so the record lies and nothing breaks. =runtime.url= · =runtime.attach= stay the root origin; =population.js:76-77= reads them.
13. =doctor.js:59-85= — =grounds(paladin.instance.daemons)= only; =runtime:= prints the slug.
14. =systems/ghost/tests/address.test.js:107-118= — routes are =<daemon>/<mode>= with a ="home"= fallback; the prototype is defined inside the test, and no verb imports it.
15. =systems/ghost/tests/delete.test.js:28,77= · =rename.test.js:28,53= — harmless; the assertions check that the file exists.

** the shift — what each noun was to the clients, what it is now

| noun | the clients assume | the runtime, after m80 · m81 |
|------+--------------------+------------------------------|
| the unit of address | a daemon slug | a host reference: =/runtime/<slug>= · =/daemon/<slug>= |
| a mode's address | =daemon.reference + /mode/t/s= | =host + /mode/t/s=, listed absolute |
| userspace | per daemon | one, the runtime's =/userspace= |
| a thread's owner | =thread.daemon= | the user; =mode= nullable; =mode → host= |
| the mode roster | =/daemon/<s>/entities/mode= | Mode rows in ONE datamap, both rungs (=daemon NULL= = the runtime) |
| the cortex | per daemon, an envelope | per host, =/cortex/<type>/<via>=; the runtime's has no route |
| ctx | anima's own =ctx.daemon= binding | the runtime's ctx is flat; anima's is untouched (R4) |
| home | none: a thread needs a daemon | the runtime rung, =/runtime/<slug>/mode/home/home= |

m80 strand G called this: /"should greatly simplify the clients as well, but that is … a concern for later. The clients are pretty shit at the moment anyways"/. The server collapsed three daemon-shaped nouns (userspace, the datamap, the address) into runtime-level ones. Anima still holds all three per daemon.

** where to rebuild, where to migrate

anima, the daemon-shaped core: REBUILD. These files are =entities/daemon/dossier.js= · =entities/thread/= · =entities/mode/mode.js= · =entities/activity.js= · =app/panels/c/cards/threads.js=.
1. A HOST dossier: =/runtime/<slug>= and every =/daemon/<slug>= on one mount sequence, a daemon being one kind of host. The roster is =[{ reference: "/runtime/<slug>" }, ...daemons]=. It is blocked on C1: the runtime serves none of the dossier's calls.
2. ONE userspace dataspace on the lighthouse connection (thread · buffer · turn · intent · activity · terminal). =thread.mode.host= replaces =thread.daemon=, and an activity's =stdin= · =stdout= wire from the root. That closes finding 4.
3. A mode is addressed by its absolute reference from =/metadata/modes= on both rungs, with =connection = root.branch(reference)=.
4. One wired root. =/metadata/aperture= already strips =/runtime/<slug>= beside =/daemon/hello= (probe step 9), so =call.runtime.<slug>.mode.home.probe.ping= is the shape a single =shape.connection.wire= gives. That candidate replaces each daemon's own =call=.
5. The Threads card mints at home with no daemon picked. Re-mode is one =updateOne= on =/userspace=.

anima, the views (panes · pincer · dock · zones): MIGRATE. They read through the core, and once the core speaks host they follow on the rename.

ghost: MIGRATE, small.
1. The doctor lists hosts: a =/runtime/<slug>= row of =runtime.modes= seats beside the daemon rows. This is tangleable on go.
2. A fixture record carrying =daemons[].modes= and =runtime.modes= through =strata.wet= catches findings 3 and 13.
3. The address prototype moves to references (=/runtime/<slug>/mode/t/s=, a relative =mode/t/s=) when ghost grows a talk verb (m79).

** the server side — RULED 10-09

beef: /"Okay, yes, absolutely. We have to rebuild Anima's core. We don't do that now. That's the next quest."/ The rebuild is scaffolded in =.ikiro/quests/m82-runtime-clients.org= (unnumbered).

| # | item | ruling |
|---+------+--------|
| C1 | the runtime host serves the daemon's metadata | LANDED 10-10 |
| C2 | one listing shape for =/metadata/modes= | LANDED 10-10 |
| C3 | the runtime's entry in the instance record says =/runtime/<slug>= | RULED yes, TANGLED into THE LAND |
| C4 | the runtime's cortex and harness, the whole capability, on routes | LANDED 10-10 |
| C5 | the scratch contract test lands in anima's suite | QUESTIONED, re-explained |

- C1 — /"the runtimes should absolutely serve metadata."/ =/runtime/<slug>/metadata/{manifest,statics,cargo,datamap,aperture,cortex,modes}=, a per-mode =/runtime/<slug>/mode/<t>/<s>/metadata/*=, =/runtime/<slug>/status=. Shape: =lifecycle/daemon/aperture/metadata.js:4-49= becomes a HOST beat run on both rungs (=die.host= on the runtime, =die.daemon= on a daemon).
- C2 — /"Yes, the listings should have the same shape."/ Both rungs list ={ type, slug, name, traits, reference, metadata }=; the daemon's rows (=metadata.js:14-21=) gain =reference=, and both are absolute.
- C3 — /"The paladin's records should say slash runtime."/, then /"I don't understand number three. What record? There's a shit ton of records in there."/ It is the runtime's entry in the instance object paladin assembles at boot from =instance.viva.json= (=paladin.instance.runtime=). =root()= fills its =reference= and =url=, and every runtime mode's =url= is built from that =url=:
  #+BEGIN_SRC diff
  // subsystems/paladin/lifecycle/resolve.js:46
     const root = (runtime) => {
       runtime.mountpoint ??= instance.paladin.scope.mountpoint.absolute;
  -    runtime.reference ??= "/";
  +    runtime.reference ??= `/runtime/${runtime.manifest?.slug}`;
       if (!reach) return;
  -    runtime.url ??= reach.absolute;
  +    runtime.url ??= reach.branch(runtime.reference).absolute;
       runtime.attach ??= reach.branch("/attached").absolute;
     };
  #+END_SRC
  Before, =paladin.instance.runtime= said ={ reference: "/", url: <origin> }= and a runtime mode's =url= was =<origin>/mode/<t>/<s>=, an address nothing serves since R6. After, it says ={ reference: "/runtime/<slug>", url: <origin>/runtime/<slug> }= (the slug ruling below; the default slug gives =/runtime/runtime=) and the mode's =url= is =<origin>/runtime/<slug>/mode/<t>/<s>=, the same shape a daemon's entry has (=resolve.js:34-36=). Readers: =runtime.url= 0 besides =population.js:76='s copy; =runtime.attach= unchanged (=traits/fraught.js:9= reads it). TANGLED into THE LAND's =resolve.js= block; replayed byte-equal; census in =* changelog=.
- C4 — /"Yes, the runtime's cortex gets a route like the daemons. Absolutely. We want the entire capability of the cortex and harness that the daemon exposes also exposed on the runtime."/ =/runtime/<slug>/cortex/<type>/{render,stream}= by =lifecycle/daemon/aperture/cortex.js:11-18= run on the host, plus the harness on a HARNESSED runtime mode at =/runtime/<slug>/mode/<t>/<s>/harness= with its =/metadata/harness=. The trait already mounts =/harness= on any host after the flatten; what is missing is the runtime's cortex route and the metadata (C1).
- C5 — /"I don't understand number five."/ Re-explained: during this campaign I wrote a test, =systems/anima/tests/runtime.contract.test.js=, on a scratch copy only. It boots a real runtime the way =systems/runtime/tests/runtime/hello.test.js= does and then calls every route anima's =entities/daemon/dossier.js:104-109= calls, in order. Today it asserts two GAPS (=.userspace= is =undefined= on a daemon's wired aperture; =/runtime/<slug>/metadata/*= 404). The question is whether to put it into anima's test folder once C1 lands, with those two steps flipped to assert the routes WORK. Then the next time the server moves a route anima reads, an anima test goes red. Today none does: =terminals.remote= serves its own roster.

Order (mine): THE LAND first, as proven. C1 · C2 · C4 next, as one tangle on the landed tree, before anima's rebuild starts, which reads them.
